- **anim**: animsmith router for the /anim command (learn, find, make, study, review, finish, port, distill, bench). Use only when the user runs /anim or names animsmith, to learn from animation references or look them up (and, in later phases, make Roblox animations). Not for general Roblox animation questions, another plugin's learn command, or editing a game's animations outside animsmith. ([zyweii/animsmith](https://github.com/zyweii/animsmith/tree/HEAD/skills/anim))
- **anim-400k-eval**: Evaluates automated end-to-end video dubbing systems by testing their ability to generate synchronized English audio from Japanese source video, specifically probing prosody matching, timing alignment, and multi-speaker isolation capabilities. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Anim-400K, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MUSHRA. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/anim-400k-eval) · ★3)

- **anim-decay**: **Genera/aplica animaciones JS frame-rate-independent usando decaimiento exponencial temporal**, para casos donde un valor persigue un objetivo que se mueve en tiempo real (drag, scroll-follow, contadores, cursores, sway) y una CSS `transition` no sirve porque el target cambia cada frame. ([AaronCoorahua/ConcordeV2](https://github.com/AaronCoorahua/ConcordeV2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anim-decay))

- **anim-engine**: Renderer-agnostic numeric animation library for JavaScript runtimes. Use when a user has installed 'anim-engine' and needs help writing animation code with tweens, keyframes, timelines, springs, smooth damp, lerp, inertia, smooth scroll, or color interpolation. Covers all exported primitives, common recipes, game engine integration, and the ticker setup pattern. ([LukeCarlThompson/anim-engine](https://github.com/LukeCarlThompson/anim-engine/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
